EXHIBIT A
SCOPE OF WORK
1.
Contractor’s Responsibilities:
Contractor shall provide all necessary labor, supervision, materials, equipment and supplies to
satisfactorily perform food and beverage concessionaire services for the City of Cheyenne at the
Dutcher Baseball Complex. The City takes pride in providing exceptional customer service to our
citizens and expects Contractor to demonstrate this same pride in the services performed. The
Contractor shall ffirnish all labor, materials, equipment, supplies, tools, services and skill required
to maintain a clean and safe environment throughout the term of the Agreement. It is the
responsibility of the Contractor to provide food and beverage concessionaire services, and obtain
permits and licenses, where applicable, from the appropriate City, State or County departments.
Contractor shall have the ability and experience to operate food and beverage concession stands at
the Dutcher Baseball Complex.
1.1.
The sale of alcoholic beverages and tobacco is strictly prohibited.
1.2.
Contractor shall have proprietary operational rights of the site. No other Contractor shall be
allowed in the Dutcher Baseball Complex or surrounding areas.
1.3.
The Contractor shall maintain a flexible schedule that accommodates game/tournament
schedules. Hours and current schedule of games/tournaments shall be provided by the City.
1.4.
From time to time, Contractor may be requested to participate in tournaments which may
include additional food and beverage concessionaire services to be provided by the Contractor. The
number of annual tournaments will be determined by the City.
1.5.
The Contractor will be required, at its sole expense, to be responsible for all alterations
necessary to accommodate equipment after having first obtained written authorization from the City
ofCheyenne. The Contractor will be required to tour and to inspect the facilities with City staffprior
to final contract negotiation and execution.
1.6.
No signs or advertising of any kind (with the exception of a menu board) shall be installed
without prior written approval of the City of Cheyenne. In addition, any sign or advertising must be
approved by the Parks Division, and be in compliance with applicable laws, codes, and ordinances.
1.7.
Contractor shall report vandalism and/or damage to the City’s property to the City Designee
immediately upon discovery.
1

1.8.
Contractor shall provide a Concession Manager who shall be responsible for the performance
of the Agreement and remain the Contractor’s contact person for the duration of the Agreement.
The Concession Manager shall establish a routine for communications with the City’s Designee to
provide a prompt and timely response to any concerns or problems that may arise. Time and
frequency of direct meetings may vary as determined by the Events Manager. The Concession
Manager shall contact the Events Manager to review overall performance, receive special
instructions regarding tournaments, or discuss other pertinent items regarding the Agreement and
the Contractor’s performance.
1.9.
Contractor shall ifirnish all supplies and materials necessary for the proper performance of
the services and operation ofthe facility unless otherwise provided. The Contractor shall not use any
material or supplies which the Events Manager determines would be unsuitable for the purpose
intended, or offensive or harmifil to any part of the facility, its contents, equipment, employees or
patrons.
1.10.
Beverages shall be either fountain drinks or served in plastic bottles. No glass bottles shall
be allowed.
1.11.
Contractor shall be responsible for cleaning and sanitizing food service area and emptying
trash receptacles inside the facility and cleaning the area directly outside ofthe facility of any trash.
Refuse, recyclables and trash from its operation shall be placed in a designated area from which the
City will then have it removed.
1.12.
Sub-contracting of the space is not permitted.
1.13.
The Contractor shall conduct its operation in a manner that does not damage City’s property.
In the event damage occurs to City’s property or any adjacent property by reason of any service
operations performed under this Agreement, the Contractor shall replace or repair the same at no
cost to the City. If damage caused by the Contractor has to be repaired or replaced by the City, the
cost of such work shall be charged to the Contractor.
1.14.
Contractor shall propose to City the prices of the products it desires to sell hereunder, and
City shall review and approve prices. It is City’s goal to have concession prices comparable with
the market prices of other comparable concession stands.
1.15.
Contractors shall comply fully with all laws, ordinances, rules and regulations ofthe United
States, State of Wyoming, County of Laramie and the City of Cheyenne, including specific City
regulations related to building permits and fees, zoning, use permit stipulations and regulations
regarding alcoholic beverages, nuisance abatement, immoral conduct, smoking!non-smoking,
privilege and use excise taxes.
2

2.
City Responsibilities:
2.1.
The City will be responsible for providing direction to the Contractor. These activities
include, but may not be limited to, the following:
a.
Identi& a Contract Administrator at the time of Contractor selection. The Contract
Administrator will submit in writing to the Contractor the names of City personnel that will have
authority to make changes or additions to the contracted items.
b.
Determine and schedule the time and frequency of direct meetings between the
Community Services Director or designee and the Contractor’s Concession Manager.
c.
Schedule inspections with the Contractor’s Concession Manager. Quality service and
strict adherence to the Agreement will be expected from the Contractor.
3
